private adj. + n. 
1 private life; private school; private hospital 
2 go to a private school NCE1L55

- Why does he go to a private school? 

conversation n.
1 have a conversation NCE1L31 & L71
- We are having a conversation. Please be quiet. 
- I had a conversation with him yesterday.
- When did you have a conversation with him? 
2 a private conversation
- This is a private conversation. 

theatre n. cinema 
1 go to a theatre NCE1L85
I’ve never been to that theatre. 
Have you ever been to that theatre? 

seat n. 
1 have a good seat
- I had a good seat.
- Why did you have a good seat? 
2 take a seat
3 Is the seat taken? 

play n. 
1 watch a play NCE1L37
I am going to watch a play with my Mum. (when) 

loudly adv. 
1 talk loudly
You are talking too loudly.
Why are you talking so loudly? 
Don’t talk so loudly in the theatre.

angry adj. angrily adv. 
1 He was very angry yesterday. 
When was he very angry?
Why was he very angry yesterday? 
2 look at her angrily
He looked at her angrily this morning.

attention
1 pay attention
They did not pay any attention. 
Did they pay any attention?

bear-bore-borne v.
1 [Story]: I could not bear it. 
I can’t bear that. 
Can you bear that? 
How can you bear that?

business
1 business man
2 It’s none of your business. 

rudely adv. rude adj. 
1 Don’t be rude. Let him in. 
2 talk rudely NCE1L117 
He was talking to her rudely. 
How was he talking to her?

# Lesson 2-1 Grammar

## 如何造⼀个英⽂句⼦?
1. 天⿊了
night ⽆主语 
2. 他们累了
They tired ⽆v. 
3. 他们在等
They wait for Godot ⽆v.变化

1 Grammar
1. 句⼦要有主语 天⿊了 
It is night. 
2. 句⼦要有v. 他们累了 
They are tired. 
3. v. 要有变化 他们在等 
They are waiting for Godot.


## 如何造⼀个好的英⽂句⼦? 
A woman is shouting. 
### +adj. / +adv.(⽅式)
An ugly woman is shouting angrily. 
A cute woman is shouting happily. 
### +地点
An ugly woman is shouting angrily in her car.
A cute woman is shouting happily on the beach. 
### +时间
An ugly woman is shouting angrily in her car at midnight.
A cute woman is shouting happily on the beach in the morning. 
### + adj. +adv.(⽅式) +时间 +地点 
An ugly woman is shouting angrily in her car at midnight. 
A cute woman is shouting happily on the beach in the morning.

• go to the theatre 
• have a good seat 
• get angry 
• turn round 
• pay attention

go to the theatre NCE1L125

have a good seat

get angry

turn round

pay attention

in the end

Where did your mum and you go in the end?

couldn’t bear it

Can you bear it?

It’s none of your business.

# Lesson 2-2 Grammer

## be+doing VS do/does 
1 名称：现在进⾏时 NCE1L31-L36 
2 含义：⼀个动作正在发⽣ 
3 结构：肯定句 amare+doing (v.现在分词) 
We are having a conversation. 
## be+doing VS do/does 
1 名称：现在进⾏时 NCE1L31-L36 
2 含义：⼀个动作正在发⽣ 
3 结构：否定句 amare not + doing 
She is not watching play. 
## be+doing VS do/does 
1 名称：现在进⾏时 NCE1L31-L36 
2 含义：⼀个动作正在发⽣ 
3 结构：⼀般疑问句 amare 提句⾸ 
Are they talking loudly? 
## be+doing VS do/does 
1 名称：现在进⾏时 
2 含义：表近期计划，安排 (红⽪书初25&中19) 
3 结构：remain the same
I am meeting my friends tonight. 
We’re having a party next weekend. 

## be+doing VS do/does 
1 名称：⼀般现在时 NCE1L47-L60 
2 含义：习惯动作/客观事实 
3 结构：肯定句 v./v. (v. 三单)
He goes to that theatre every weekend. 
They go to that theatre every weekend. 
## be+doing VS do/does 
1 名称：⼀般现在时 NCE1L47-L60 
2 含义：习惯动作/客观事实 
3 结构：否定句 do/does + not +v.
He doesn’t go to that theatre every weekend. 
They don’t go to that theatre every weekend. 
## be+doing VS do/does 
1 名称：⼀般现在时 NCE1L47-L60 
2 含义：习惯动作/客观事实 
3 结构：⼀般疑问句 do/does 提句⾸
Does he go to that theatre every weekend? 
Do they go to that theatre every weekend? 

---

## What 感叹句 结构：
What + (形容词) + n. + (主语+谓语) 
It is a terrible day.
What a terrible day (it is)! 
This is a beautiful picture.
What a beautiful picture this is! 
This is a wonderful garden.
What a wonderful garden this is! 
What A Wonderful World 
  
They are wonderful actors.
What wonderful actors they are! 
She is a hard-working woman. 
What a hard-working woman she is! 
It is a tall building!
What a tall building it is! 
You are a clever boy.
What a clever boy you are! 
She is a pretty girl.
What a pretty girl she is! 
It’s a terrible film. 
What a terrible film it is! 

频率 adv. 的位置
always & usually & sometimes etc. 
1. v.前
2. be 后
3. 切勿当作⾦科⽟律。例外? 错了的后果?
4. 靠⼤量输⼊ VS 背诵

# 2-3 Grammar
## was & were & did 
1 名称：⼀般过去时 NCE1L67-L70 
2 含义：过去的状态
3 结构：肯定句 was were 
He was very angry yesterday. 
4 结构：否定句 was were+not
He wasn’t very angry yesterday. 
5 结构：⼀般疑问句 was were 提句⾸ 
Was he very angry yesterday? 
## 1 名称：⼀般过去时 NCE1L71-L78 
2 含义：⼀个动作发⽣在过去
3 结构：肯定句 v.ed (v.过去式) 
I had breakfast this morning. 
4 结构：否定句 did+not & v.变原型
I didn’t have breakfast this morning. 
5 结构：⼀般疑问句did提句⾸&v.变原型 
Did you have breakfast this morning?

# 2-4 Grammar
## have+done ⽤法⼀ 
名称：现在完成时 NCE1L83-L88 
含义：过去的事情对现在产⽣了影响 (⾔之过去，意在当下) 
## 结构
### 肯定句 have/has+done 
A: I can help to clean his shoes. 
B: He has cleaned his shoes. (His shoes are clean now) 

A: Do you want to have dinner with us? 
B: I have just had some food. (I’m full now.) 
A: Are they at home?
B: They have gone out. (They are not at home now.)

### 否定句 have/has+not+done 
I haven’t had breakfast. 
⼀般疑问句 have/has提前&done不变 
Have you had breakfast? 

## have+done ⽤法⼀ 
### have+done=did+do/does 
A: I can help to clean his shoes.
B: He has cleaned his shoes.(His shoes are clean now)
He cleaned his shoes, and now his shoes are clean. 
### have+done=did+do/does 
A: Do you want to have dinner with us? 
B: I have just had some food. (I’m full now.)
I had some food, and now I’m full. 
### have+done=did+do/does 
A: Are they at home?
B: They have gone out. (They are not at home now.)
They went out, and now they are not at home. 

## have+done ⽤法⼀ 
名称：现在完成时 NCE1L83-L88 
含义：过去的事情对现在产⽣了影响 (⾔之过去，意在当下) 
结构：肯定句 have/has+done 
1. have+done=did+do/does 
2. have+done≈did 


## have+done ⽤法⼆ 
名称：现在完成时 NCE1L89-L90 
含义：从过去持续到现在的事情 
## 结构：
### 肯定句 have/has+done
I have lived in Beijing for 10 years/since 2012. 
I have lived in Beijing for 10 years. 
I lived in Beijing for 10 years. 
   
### 否定句 have/has+not+done
I haven’t eaten anything since breakfast.(from breakfast until now) 
⼀般疑问句 have/has提前&done不变
Have you heard anything from Berlin since yesterday?

# 2-5 Grammar

| 用法 | 描述发生在过去的<br>动作(did)或状态(was/were) NCE1L67–78 |
| :---: | :---: |
| 结构<br>did<br>was/were<br>v.ed<br>(过去式) | 肯定 | Tom watched TV last night.<br>Emily was at the office this morning. |
| | 否定 | Tom didn’t watch TV last night.<br>Emily wasn’t at the office this morning. |
| | 一般疑问 | Did Tom watch TV last night?<br>Was Emily at the office this morning? |
| | 难点 | 否定&疑问v.<br>肯定: v.ed (变化规则VS无规则) |


| 用法1 | 言之过去，意在当下 NCE2L4 |
| :---: | :---: |
| 结构<br>have+done<br>v.ed<br>done<br>过去分词 | 肯定 | A: Do you want to have dinner?<br>B: I have just had some food.(I’m full now.) |
| | 否定 | have/has + not |
| | 一般疑问 | have/has 提句首 |
| | 难点 | have done VS did<br>v.ed(done 过去分词) |


have+done ⽤法⼀ 
have+done=did+do/does
A: I can help to clean his shoes.
B: He has cleaned his shoes. (His shoes are clean now)
He cleaned his shoes, and now his shoes are clean. 

have+done ⽤法⼀ 
名称：现在完成时 NCE1L83-L88 
含义：过去的事情对现在产⽣了影响 (⾔之过去，意在当下) 
结构：肯定句 have/has+done 
1. have+done=did+do/does 
2. have+done≈did 

## 现在完成时

| 用法2 | 一个动作 done 或者状态 been<br>从过去持续或重复到现在 NCE2L4 |
| :--- | :--- |
| 结构<br>have+done<br>v.ed<br>done<br>过去分词 | 肯定： I have lived in Beijing for 10 years/since 2012.<br>I have been here for 6 years. |
| | 否定： have/has + not |
| | 一般疑问： have/has 提句首 |
| | 特殊疑问： How long have you lived in Beijing? |
| | 难点： have lived VS lived?<br>v. 可持续<br>v.ed (done 过去分词) |

# 2-8 Grammar
## adj.⽐较级&最⾼级-构成
1. 单⾳节：+er/est
small → smaller → smallest 
new → newer → newest 
old → older → oldest
2. 单⾳节：以e结尾 +r/st 
large → larger → largest 
nice → nicer → nicest
3. 单⾳节：元+辅结尾，双写辅⾳字⺟，加er, est 
big → bigger → biggest
hot → hotter → hottest
fat → fatter → fattest
4. 双⾳节：辅⾳字⺟+y结尾的，把y变为i，加er, est
pretty → prettier → prettiest
easy → easier → easiest
heavy → heavier → heaviest
补充: 
famous → more famous → the most famous 
clever → cleverer/more clever → cleverest/ the most clever 
5. 不规则
good/well → better → best 
bad → worse → worst
much /many → more → most 
little → less → least 
6. 多⾳节adj.(⼤于等于3)，前加more, most表肯定
beautiful => more beautiful => the most beautiful 
intelligent => more intelligent => the most intelligent 
7. 多⾳节adj.(⼤于等于3)，前加less, least表否定
beautiful => less beautiful => the least beautiful 
expensive => less expensive => the least expensive 


## adj.⽐较级&最⾼级-⽤法
### 1. 主语+be+adj.er+than+⽐较对象(I formal/ me informal) 
The left one is smaller than the right one. 
Yao is taller than Jet Li. 
The left one is prettier than the right one. 
### 2. 主语+be+the+adj.est +n.+范围 
我爸爸是全世界最酷(coolest)的⼈。
My father is the coolest guy in the world. 
世上只有妈妈好(kindest)。
My mom is the kindest person in the world. 
我的邻居是全世界最好(nicest)的⼈。
My neighbor is the nicest guy in the world. 
### 3. 主语+be+the+adj.est+n.+I’ve ever met/seen 
特蕾莎修⼥是我知道最善良的⼈。
Mother Teresa is the kindest woman I’ve ever known. 
姚明是我⻅过最⾼的⼈。
Yao Ming is the tallest guy I’ve ever seen. 
卓别林是我⻅过最有趣的⼈。
Charles Chaplin is the funniest guy I’ve ever seen.

## 被动-be+done 被动有时态吗?

am/is/are+done

am/is/are+done

was/were+done

was/were+done

was/were+done

amare+being+done

amare+being+done

amare+being+done

The computer ____ ____ ____ at the moment. 

have/has been done

have/has been done

# 2-12 Grammar
## ⼀般将来时: will-shall
含义: 将 / 会 肯定句 主+will+do+其他
It is 11 o’clock now. Sarah is at work.
At 11 o’clock yesterday, she was at work.
At 11 o’clock tomorrow, she will be at work. 
I’ll  We’ll ... 

### ⼀般将来时: will-shall 含义: 将 / 会 (当机⽴断的决定) 肯定句 
主+ will + do + 其他 It’s too heavy.
I’ll carry it for you. 
### ⼀般将来时: will-shall 含义: 将 / 会 否定句 
主+will + not (won’t)
I’m sorry I was late this morning. 
It won’t happen again. 
### ⼀般将来时: will-shall 含义: 将 / 会 ⼀般疑问句 
Will + 主 + do ...? 
Will you help me? 

## ⼀般将来时: will-shall
肯定句: I/We+ shall + do + 其他 
否定句: I/We+ shall + not(shan’t) /ʃɑːnt/
⼀般疑问句: Shall + I/we ...

# 2-15 Grammar
## 宾语从句 
I believe you./ I believe (that) you are right. 
⼀个句⼦做宾语，这个句⼦叫宾语从句 NCE1 L99-102; L133-136 

宾语从句 
I think that we’ll have plenty of time. L12
I think that he’ll never pay it back. L11
I believe that they will set out tomorrow. L12
He said that our teacher refused to help us. L9 
She told me that a famous pop singer moved to our street. L6 
直接引语 — 间接引语 
直接引语: “I am busy”, he said. 
间接引语: He said that he was busy. 
间接引语: He says that he is busy. 
注意: 
1.⼈称变化 
2.从句时态变化
3. 添加连词(宾从连词 that) 
直接引语: “I never work on Sundays”, 
she said. 
间接引语: She said that she never 
worked on Sundays. 
间接引语: She says that she never 
works on Sundays. 
直接引语 — 间接引语 
从句时态变化 
amwere
amwere doing 
does/do — did
have/has done — had done
did — had done 
直接引语 — 间接引语 
从句情态动词变化 
can-could 
may-might 
will-would 
shall-should 
must-had to

# 2-16 Grammar
## if 引导的条件从句 
Shall we go by bus or taxi?
If we go by bus, it will be cheaper.
We will get there more quickly if we go by taxi. 
If we go there by bus, it will be cheaper.
It will be cheaper if we go by bus. 
**If从句可前可后。**
## 主将从现 
If you don’t hurry, you’ll miss the train. 
You’ll miss the train if you don’t hurry. 
If he is out, I’ll call tomorrow.
I’ll call tomorrow if he is out. 
## 主将从现 
If you give us 20 minutes, we will give you 10 years back.
If I don’t feel well tomorrow, I’ll stay at home. 
## 主情从现 
If you need money, I can lend you some. 
## 主祈从现 
If you don’t like it, don’t eat it.

# 2-19 Grammar
## may / might 表现在推测 对现在不确信的肯定(否定)推测。 “也许是” (也许不是) 
may/might (not) do  
may/might (not) be adj./n.  
may/might (not) be doing

You are looking for Bob.
Nobody is sure where he is but you get some suggestions.
You: Where's Bob?
He may be in his office. (= perhaps he is in his office) 

You are looking for Bob. 
Nobody is sure where he is but you get some suggestions.
You: Where's Bob?
He might be having lunch. (= perhaps he is having lunch) 

You are looking for Bob. 
Nobody is sure where he is but you get some suggestions.
You: Where's Bob?
Ask Ann. She might know. (= perhaps she knows) 

(It’s possible that I won’t have time to meet you) 
I might not have time to meet you. 

## may / might 表现在推测 对过去不确信的肯定(否定)推测。 “当时也许是”(当时也许不是) 
may/might (not) have done.  
may/might (not) have been adj./n.  
may/might (not) have been doing.

A: I wonder why Kay didn't answer the phone.
B: She may have been asleep. (= perhaps she was asleep) 

A: I can't find my bag anywhere.
B: You might have left it in the shop. (= perhaps you left it in the shop) 

A: I wonder why Colin was in such a bad mood yesterday.
B: He may not have been feeling well. (= perhaps he wasn't feeling well)

# 2-25 Grammar
## 并列连词conj. 
1. and, but, or, so
2. both... and, either... or, neither... nor 
3. not only... but also (as well) 

### 并列连词conj. 
1. and, but, or, so 
同⼀主语情况下，主语不必重复
He finished lunch and (he) went into 
the garden. Jim speaks French but his 
wife speaks English. 
### 并列连词conj. 
2. both... and, either... or, neither... nor 
Both Leo and his GF like to watch movies. 
Both my wife and I are going to Pairs. 
连接主语 1
He can both sing and dance. 
连接谓语 2
I can speak both English and German. 
连接宾语 3

### 并列连词conj. 
2. both... and, either... or, neither... nor
Either the teacher or the students ______ wrong. 
连接主语 1: v. 就近⼀致 (are)
When the girl is happy, she either sings or dances. 
连接谓语 2
I want either this red one or that green one. 
连接宾语 3 

### 并列连词conj. 
2. both... and, either... or, neither... nor 
Neither dad nor mum ___ at home today. 
连结主语 1: v.就近原则 (is)
When the boy is sad, he neither sings nor dances. 
连接谓语 2
I want neither this one nor that one. 
连接宾语 3

### 并列连词conj. 
not only… but also (as well) 
不仅…⽽且…
Not only the doctor but also the patients _________ know about it. (do not)
连结主语 1:  v.就近原则 (don’t)
You must not only wash the dishes but sweep the floor as well. (= but also sweep the floor.)
连接谓语 2
Shakespeare was not only a writer but also an actor.
连接宾语 3

# 2-28 Grammar

输出定从

- ⼀句汉语变两句英⽂
- 确定被修饰词
- 确定who/whom/which/that
- 确定是否可以省略（替代宾语）
- who/whom/which/that替代被修饰词，并提句⾸
- 定从紧随被修饰词之后

### 住在隔壁的男⼈是⼀个医⽣。
The man is a doctor.
He lives next door. (who lives next door) 
The man who lives next door is a doctor. 
### 我们认识许多住在伦敦的⼈。
We know a lot of people.
They live in London. (who live in London)
We know a lot of people who live in London.
### 发⽣故障的那个机器已经被修理好了。
The machine has been repaired.
The machine broke down. (which broke down)
The machine which broke down has been repaired.
### 我想⻅的那个⼥⼈去度假了。
The woman was away on holiday.
I wanted to see that woman. (who I wanted to see)
The woman who I wanted to see was away on holiday. 
### whose引导的定从 
We saw some people.
Their car had broken down.
We saw some people whose car had broken down. 
### whose引导的定从 
I met a man.
His wife is an English teacher.
I met a man whose wife is a English teacher. 
### whose引导的定从 
I met somebody.
His parents work in a bank.
I met somebody whose parents work in a bank. 
### whose引导的定从 
I met somebody.
Her mother writes stories.
I met somebody whose mother writes stories.

过去完成时被动

# 3-37 Grammar
## 将来完成时 - ⽤法1 
⽤法1: ⼀个动作持续或重复到将来 (have done的将来时) 
We will have taken five exams by next Friday.
肯定: We will have taken five exams by next Friday. 
否定: will + not = won’t
⼀般疑问句: will 提句⾸ 
We will have taken five exams by next Friday. 
• Ted and Amy have been married for 24 years.
• Next year they will have been married for 25 years.
• When their son was born, they had been married for three years.



## 将来完成时 - ⽤法2 
⽤法2: ⼀个动作截⽌到未来某个时间前完成 
肯定: I will have finished my homework by next Friday. 
否定: will + not = won’t
⼀般疑问句: will 提句⾸

# 2-38 Grammar
## had done 过去完成时 含义: 过去的过去
no sooner ... than / hardly ... when

### no sooner ... than
He had no sooner returned than he bought a house and settled down in the country. 
⼀...就...
主 had no sooner done than +句⼦(did) 

### hardly ... when 
He had hardly had time to settle down when he sold the house and left the country. 
⼏乎刚...就
主 + had hardly done when + 句⼦(did) 

### had done 
⼀...就...
主 had no sooner done than +句⼦(did) 
⼏乎刚...就 
主 + had hardly done when + 句⼦(did)
He entered the apartment.
He fell on the floor.
He had no sooner entered the apartment than he fell on the floor. 
He had hardly entered the apartment when he fell on the floor. 

### had done ⼀...就... 
主 had no sooner done than +句⼦(did) 
I arrived at the station.
The train came.
I had no sooner arrived at the station than the train came. 

### had done ⼀...就... 
主 had no sooner done than +句⼦(did) 
We heard the noise.
We rushed to the spot.
We had no sooner heard the noise 
than we rushed to the spot.

### had done ⼀...就... 
主 had no sooner done than +句⼦(did) 
She finished on project.
She started working on the next.
She had no sooner finished one 
project than she started working on the next.

### had done ⼀...就... 
主 had no sooner done than +句⼦(did) 
I closed my eyes.
I fell asleep.
I had no sooner closed my eyes than I fell asleep.

### had done 
⼏乎刚...就
主 + had hardly done when + 句⼦(did) 
I had reached the station.
The train came.
I had hardly reached the station when the train came.

# 2-40 Grammar
## 虚拟语⽓
结构: If +did/were，
主 + would/should/could/might do.
**did 并⾮表示过去**
If I were you, I wouldn’t do that.
If I knew them better, I might invite them to dinner. 
## 含义:
1. if从句中v.与现在事实相反 
2. 说话⼈认为，if从句中v.发⽣的⼏率⾮常低
If I were you, I wouldn’t do that.
If I knew them better, I might invite them to dinner. 
It’s cold. If I were you, I would put my coat on.

# 2-41 Grammar
need
I need your help.
Do you need my help? 
She needs an apple. 
Does she need an apple?


needn’t have done 本不必如此. 但事情已经发⽣了，有后悔的语⾔效果。
George went out. He took an umbrella because he thought it was going to rain. But it didn’t rain.
He ____________ an umbrella. (needn’t have taken) 

Ann bought some eggs when she went shopping. When she got home, she 
found that she already had plenty of eggs.
She ___________ any eggs. (needn’t have bought) 

A friend got angry with you and shouted at you.You think this was unnecessary. Later you say to him/her.
You ___________ at me. (needn’t have shouted) 

Brain had no money, so he sold his car. A few days later he won some 
money in a lottery. He ____________ his car. (needn’t have sold) 

need 
She needs to sleep.
Does she need to sleep? 
Why does she need to sleep? 
You don’t have to do that. 
You don’t need to do that.

need 
You needn’t do that.

When we went on holiday ,we took the camera with us but we didn’t use it in 
the end.
We __________ the camera with us. (needn’t have taken) 

I thought I was going to miss my train so I rushed to the station. But the 
train was late and in the end I had to wait 20 minutes.
I ___________ to the station. (needn’t have rushed) 
need

Why did you wash that shirt? It wasn’t dirty.
You ___________ it. (needn’t have washed) 

Leo bought some presents for you. And give them to you. 
You say，“you ___________ all these.” (needn’t have bought)

# 4-43 Grammar
## be able to do sth. 含义 
can VS amare able to do sth.
We can see the lake from our hotel.
We are able to see the lake from our hotel. 
## be able to do sth. 时态 
・amare able to do sth.
We are able to see the lake from our hotel. 
・was/were able to do sth.
We were able to see the lake from our hotel. 
・have/has been able to do sth.
I haven’t been able to sleep recently. 
・will be able to do sth.
He will be able to help you tonight.

### could VS was able to do 
・过去能⼒: could & was able to
1. My grandfather could speak five languages.
2. My grandfather was able to speak five languages. 
### could VS was able to do 
・过去成功事件(succeeded in doing sth.): was able to 
1. The fire spread quickly, but everybody was able to escape.
2. Jack and Andy played a match yesterday. 
Andy played well, but Jack was able to beat him. 
3. a: Did you finish your work this afternoon?
b: Yes, there was nobody to disturb me, so I ______.

# 2-49 Grammar
## 分词作状语 
1. 分词: doing VS done 
amare + doing NCE1 L31 
was/were + doing NCE2 L7 
have + done NCE1 L83-90 
was/were + done NCE1 L141-144

2. 分词 (doing & done) 作定语 

| 形式 | 语态/时态含义 | 例句 / 解释 |
| :--- | :--- | :--- |
| done<br>作定语<br>L29 | 被动一般<br>被动完成<br>主动完成 | • The ‘taxi’ is a small Swiss airplane called a ‘Pilatus Porter’.<br>• on a ploughed field<br>• a retired captain = a captain who has retired |
| doing<br>作定语<br>L30 | 主动进行<br>主动一般 | • the sleeping child = the child who is sleeping<br>• I like the girl dancing with that guy. |


|类别|规则/说明|
|--|--|
|分<br>词<br>作<br>状<br>语|1. 前后主语⼀致(例外？)<br>2. 去掉状从主语 & 连词(可留)<br>3. 从句v.变分词<br>doing 主动进⾏/主动⼀般<br>done  被动完成/被动⼀般|


3. 分词(doing & done)作状语: 简化状从
The baby opened his eyes.
The baby began to cry.
When the baby opened his eyes, he began to cry.
(When) opening his eyes, the baby began to cry.

## doing 作状语  
3. 分词(doing & done)作状语: 简化状从 
She felt tired. 
She went to bed early. 
Because she felt tired, she went to bed early.  
Feeling tired, she went to bed early. 

3. 分词(doing & done)作状语: 简化状从 
He got down on one knee. 
He asked her to marry him. 
After he got down on one knee, he asked her to marry him.  
(After) Getting down on one knee, he asked her to marry him.  

3. 分词(doing & done)作状语: 简化状从  
He was rushing out of the room. 
He was knocked down by a car. 
As he was rushing out of the room, he was knocked down by a car.  
Rushing out of the room, he was knocked down by a car.  

3. 分词(doing & done)作状语: 简化状从  
等⻋的时候，⼀块砖砸到我头上了 
Waiting for a bus, a brick fell on my head. ❌  
While I was waiting for a bus, a brick fell on my head. 

3. 分词(doing & done)作状语: 简化状从  
We arrived at the party. 
We saw Ruth. 
When we arrived at the party, we saw Ruth. Arriving at the party, we saw Ruth.

3. 分词(doing & done)作状语: 简化状从 
He took off his shoes. 
Ray walked into the house. 
After he took off his shoes, Ray walked into the house.  
Taking off his shoes, Ray walked into the house. 

3. 分词(doing & done)作状语:简化状从 
L45: Sam Benton, the local butcher,  
had lost his wallet while taking his savings to the post office. 
... while he was taking his savings to the post office.  
Sam Benton, the local butcher, had lost his wallet. 
He was taking his savings to the post office.

## done 作状语  
3. 分词(doing & done)作状语:简化状从 
The city was destroyed during the war. 
The city has now been rebuilt. 
Although the city was destroyed during the war, the city has now been rebuilt.  
Destroyed during the war, the city has now been rebuilt.

3. 分词(doing & done)作状语:简化状从 
It is looked after carefully. 
The plant can live through the winter. 
If it is looked ..., the plant can ... 
Looked after carefully, the plant can live through the winter.

3. 分词(doing & done)作状语:简化状从 
The house was built of wood. 
It was clearly a fire risk. 
As the house was built of wood, it was clearly a fire risk.  
Built of wood, the house was clearly a fire risk. 

## doing VS done 
3. 分词(doing & done)作状语: 简化状从  
The teacher came in.
The teacher was followed by students.
The teacher came in, followed by students.
The teacher came in.
The teacher followed the students.
The teacher came in, following the students.

3. 分词(doing & done)作状语: 简化状从  
The car had an accident, hitting a train.  
The car had an accident, hit by a train.  

## having done
3. 分词(doing & done)作状语: 简化状从
He had completed the book.
He had a holiday.
After he had completed the book, he had a holiday. (After) 
Having completed the book, he had a holiday.
After completing the book, he had a holiday.

3. 分词(doing & done)作状语:简化状从 
I had already seen it twice. 
I didn’t want to see it again. 
Because I had already seen it twice, I didn’t want to see it again.  
Having already seen it twice, I didn’t want to see it again.

3. 分词(doing & done)作状语:简化状从 
Having stayed in his room all day, he wanted to go out for some fresh air.
Because he had stayed in his room...
He had stayed in his room all day.
He wanted to go out for some fresh air.

3. 分词(doing & done)作状语:简化状从 
L20: After having spent whole morning on the river,  
I always go home with an empty bag. 
After I have spent whole morning ... 
I have spent whole morning on the river. 
I always go home with an empty bag. 

## not doing 
3. 分词(doing & done)作状语: 简化状从
I did not want to wake her.
I left the house silently.
Because I didn’t want to wake her, I left the house silently.
Not wanting to wake her, I left the house silently.

3. 分词(doing & done)作状语: 简化状从
I didn’t know what to do.
I asked him for help.
Since I didn’t know what to do, I asked him for help.
Not knowing what to do, I asked him for help.

3. 分词(doing & done)作状语: 简化状从 
I didn’t expect anyone to be in the house. 
I walked straight in. 
Because I didn’t expect ..., I walked... 
Not expecting anyone to be in the house, I walked straight in.

3. 分词(doing & done)作状语: 简化状从
Not knowing what to do, I telephoned the police.
Because I didn’t know what to do, I telephoned the police.
I didn’t know what to do.
I telephoned the police.

## be + adj. + n.  分词作状语
3. 分词(doing & done)作状语: 简化状从
I am sorry.
Sorry.
I am fine, thank you.
Fine, thank you.
be 不翻译

3. 分词(doing & done)作状语: 简化状从
They were excited.
They played video games.
(Being) excited, they played video games.
Excited, they played video games.

3. 分词(doing & done)作状语: 简化状从
My girlfriend is a doctor.
She knows what medicine you should take.
(Being) a doctor, my girlfriend knows what medicine you should take.

3. 分词(doing & done)作状语: 简化状从 
When I was ten years old, my family moved to the city.  
When ten years old, my family moved to the city. ❌

he smashed everything in the kitchen to pieces.

prefer: like, choose, or want one thing rather than another

prefer: like, choose, or want one thing rather than another

would prefer

would prefer

rather than go to the cinema.

# 2-52 Grammar
## 现在完成进⾏时 
## ⽤法⼀: 过去进⾏的v.(结束)对现在有影响 
结构
主 + have/has been doing sth.
主 + have/has + not + been doing sth.
Have/Has + 主 + been doing sth. ?
What have/has + 主 + been doing?

## ⽤法⼀: 过去进⾏的v.(结束)对现在有影响 
肯定句: have been doing
Paul is very tired. 
He has (=He’s) been working very hard. (he’s tired now) 

Your eyes look red. What happened? 
I have been watching TV

Is it raining? No, but the ground is wet.  
It has been raining.

## ⼀般疑问句: Have/Has+主+been doing sth.? 
You’re out of breath.
Have you been running? (you’re out of breath now).

John looks sunburnt.
You ask: (you/ sit in the sun?)
Have you been sitting in the sun?

## 特殊疑问句: What have/has+主+been doing?
You meet a friend in the street. His face and hands are very dirty. 
You ask: (what/ you/ do?)
What have you been doing? 

## ⽤法⼆: 过去进⾏的v.持续(重复)到现在，也许还会继续。(同have + done⽤法⼆) 
结构
主 + have/has been doing sth.
It is raining now.
It began raining two hours ago and it is still raining.
How long has it been raining?
It has been raining for two hours.
Tim is still watching TV.
He’s been watching TV all day.

现在完成进⾏时 
She has been painting her bedroom. 
强调动作本身，动作完成与否不重要
She has painted her bedroom. 
强调动作结果，已经完成。

# 2-55 Grammar
## would do VS used to do 
L31: used to do sth. would 过去重复发⽣的动作 
## 异1: 过去状态(past state)只⽤ used to do
1. This sort of novel used to be very popular. 
2. I used to have long hair.
3. He used to be the head of a very large business company. 
   
## 异2: used to do 暗含现在不发⽣了
1. He used to/would bring me little presents. 
## 异3: ⽤used to do ⽽⾮ would 谈论[经常]且[重要]的[习惯⾏为]。— Michael Swan “Practical English Usage” 
1. Robert used to play a lot of football.
2. I used to smoke.
3. I would smoke. ??? 
注意: 
1. 并不很重要 
2. ⼤量输⼊提升语感 
3. 语法书和⼈们使⽤习惯的区别

## would do VS used to do 
L31: used to do sth. would 过去重复发⽣的动作 
同: 
1. When Leo was angry, he would always walk out of the room.
2. I used to live near my work and would always get home early.

where whose
定从 参考L28

where/whose 引导定从 
•The hotel wasn’t very clean.
•We stayed there.
•The hotel (where we stayed) wasn’t very clean. 

where/whose 引导定从 
• I recently went back to the town. 
• I was born in that town.
• I recently went back to the town (where I was born). 
 
where/whose 引导定从 
• I want to live in the country.
• There is plenty of sunshine in that country.
• I want to live in the country where there is plenty of sunshine. 
  
where/whose 引导定从 
• The place (where we spent our holidays) was really beautiful.
• The place was really beautiful. 
• We spent out holiday there. 
 
where/whose 引导定从 
• A cemetery is a place where people are buried. 
• I can’t remember the name of the garage where we had the car repaired .
• Ann bought a dress which didn’t fit her, so she took it back to the shop where Ann bought it.

where/whose 引导定从 
Yellow Submarine
In the town where I was born, Lived a man who sailed the sea. 
And he told us of his life, In the land of submarines.

where/whose 引导定从 
I have a dream (that my four little children will one day live in a nation [where they will not be judged by the color of their skin but by the content of their character]). 
我梦想有⼀天，我的四个孩⼦将在⼀个不是以他们的肤⾊，⽽是以他们的品格优劣来评价他们的国度⾥⽣活。

where/whose 引导定从 
• We helped some people.
• Their car had broken down.
• We helped some people whose car had broken down. 
 
where/whose 引导定从
• I met a man.
• His wife is an English teacher.
• I met a man whose wife is an English teacher.

where/whose 引导定从 
An orphan is a child whose parents are dead.

where/whose 引导定从 
• I met a man.
• He knows you.
• I met a man who knows you.
• I met a man.
• His sister knows you.
• I met a man whose sister knows you.

where/whose 引导定从
• A man (whose accomplishments in South Africa... will always be remembered). Gandhi (1982)
• ...from a guy whose wife is missing. Gone Girl (2014)
• A child whose mother and father have been killed. Gandhi (1982)

# 2-58 Grammar

## 据说 be said to do sth. = it is said that...
•He is the richest man in the world.
•He is said to be the richest man in the world.
•It is said that he is the richest man in the world.

•People say he is a genius.
•He is said to be a genius.
•It’s said that he is a genius.

• Cathy works very hard.
• It is said that she works 16 hours a day.
• She is said to work 16 hours a day.

• Cathy loves running.
• It is said that she runs ten miles a day.
• She is said to run ten miles a day.
• The tiny village of Frinley is said to possess a 'cursed tree’.
• It is said that the tiny village of Frinley possesses a 'cursed tree'

He is said to be the wisest man in the universe. Go, find him. 
The Last Dragon (1985)

It is said that we humans use only a tiny portion of our brains. 
Matilda (1996)

It is said that music... is a bridge between earth and heaven. 
The Forbidden Kingdom (2008)

## 强调句 it is(was)+强调部分+that(who)+剩余部分
### My secretary sent the bill to Mr. Harding yesterday.
1. It was my secretary that sent the bill to Mr. Harding yesterday.
2. It was the bill that my secretary sent to Mr. Harding yesterday.
3. It was Mr. Harding that my secretary sent the bill to yesterday.
4. It was yesterday that my secretary sent the bill to Mr. Harding.

### Sue sent Jim a present at Christmas.
✓ It was Sue that sent Jim a present at Christmas. 
✓ It was Jim that Sue sent a present at Christmas. 
✓ It was a present that Sue sent Jim at Christmas. 
✓ It was at Christmas that Sue sent Jim a present. 
 
### My aunt took Peter to London yesterday.
• It was my aunt who took Peter to London yesterday, not my mother.
• It was Peter that my aunt took to London yesterday, not Lucy.
• It was London that my aunt took peter to yesterday.
• It was yesterday that my aunt took peter to London.

[Story]: It is only in recent years that it has gained an evil reputation.
It has gained an evil reputation only in recent years.
• It is the tree that has gained an evil reputation only in recent years.
• It is an evil reputation that it has gained only in recent years.
• It is only in recent years that it has gained an evil reputation.

• ‘Helen bought the car from Tom.’
• ‘No, it was Tom that bought the car from Helen.’ 
• ‘No, it was two years ago that Helen bought the car.’ 
• ‘No, it was Tom that Helen bought the car from.’

# 2-59 Grammar
## ⽬的状语从句 to / in order to / so as to / so that / in order that
### so that 以便; 为了
1. I wrote them down in my diary so that I wouldn't have to remember.

2. Everybody is having a good time here, but I think we should leave you alone... so that you can relax.

3. He wants to move out of his place so that we can live together.

4. Take a seat so that we may continue.

5. I should tell you, so that you don't waste your time... you can't make me angry.

6. Put your name on the top of the paper... so that I know who did such good work. 
7. Now, my sisters saved up so that I could go to school and be a teacher.
8. Sometimes we have to leave people behind so that we can go on, so that we can continue to fight.
9. I'd like you to leave so that I can take a bath.
10. You will watch this, so that you will remember.

# 2-62 Grammar
## 过去完成进⾏时
have been doing... L52 
will have been doing... L61 
had been doing...

## 过去完成进⾏时 
肯定句: 主+had been doing... 
否定句: 主+had not been doing... 
⼀般疑问句: Had 主 been doing...? 
⽤法: 现在完成进⾏时的过去时


### ⽤法⼀: 过去A进⾏的v. (结束)对过去B有影响。 
•Yesterday morning I got up and looked out of the window. The sun was shining but the ground was very wet. 
•It had been raining. 

She had been crying. Her eyes were red. 
She had been running. She was out of breath. 
I had been working all day. 
When I got home, I was very tired.

### ⽤法⼆: 过去A进⾏的v.持续或重复到过去B
• At last the bus came.
• I’d been waiting for 20 minutes.
By 2020, I had been teaching English for 9 years. Past A 2020 Now

# 2-64 Grammar
## 虚拟语⽓ L40
- 结构
If+did/were，
主 + wouldmight do. 
did 并⾮表示过去
If I were you, I wouldn’t do that.
If I knew them better, I might invite them to dinner.
- 含义
1. if从句中v.与现在事实相反 
2. 说话⼈认为，if 从句中 v. 发⽣的⼏率⾮常低
If I were you, I wouldn’t do that.
If I knew them better, I might invite them to dinner. 
It’s cold. If I were you, I would put my coat on. 

## 虚拟语⽓: 与过去事实相反
▪ Police: Why didn’t you shout for help when you were robbed of your watch. 
▪ Man: If I had opened (open) my mouth, they would have found (find) my four gold teeth. That would be much worse. 

## 虚拟语⽓ L64
结构: If 主 had done..., 主 would/could/ might/should + have done.  
L64 与过去事实相反
▪I didn’t see you when you passed me in the street. If I had seen you, of course, I would have said hello.
▪I didn’t go out last night. I would have gone out if I hadn’t been so tired.
▪If I’d gone to the party last night, I would have met lots of people.

I don't know what I would have done if you hadn't come back. 
Falcon Song (2014) 
If I'd known how much fun it would be to work with a partner, I would have done it years ago. 
The Incredible Burt Wonderstone (2013)

It would have helped if you'd saved my son. 
The Entitled (2011) 

## 虚拟语⽓ L64 
结构: If 主 had done..., 主 would/could/ might/should + have done.  
L64 与过去事实相反
If you could have made (=If you had been able to make)
If you could have

spoken to me the first time in the jungle, what would you have said? Oh! 
The Jungle Book (1994)

If you could have done (=If you had been able to do) 
If I could have stayed down there, I would have, believe me. 
The Spectacular Now (2013)

If you could have done (=If you had been able to do) 
You know, Jammy, if I could have done anything, I would have been a painter. 
Trespass Against Us (2016)

对⽐ (L40 VS L64) 虚拟语⽓
L40: If + did/were， 
主 + would/should/could/might do.  
L64: If 主 had done..., 主 would have done.  
I’m not hungry. 
If I was hungry, I would eat something. 
I wasn’t hungry,  
If I had been hungry, I would have eaten something.

**注意!**
▪规则与例外
▪1990s less than 25% of if ... follow the traditional 123.
▪It may not strictly speaking be wrong.

# 2-64 Story+Expressions
if 真实条件句
If jack didn’t come to work yesterday, he was probably ill.
If you didn’t study English at school, you won’t understand this book.

suggest that +句⼦ 
suggest that...should do 
考试: 
Her uncle suggested that she (should) get a job in a bank.
He suggests that she get a job in a bank.
He suggested that she get a job in a bank. 
实际应⽤: 
Her uncle suggests that she gets a job in a bank. 
He suggested that she got a job in a bank.

• it is possible to do sth. ... 
• a serve as b
• put forward a plan...
• solve the problem of a…


it is possible to do sth. ...
L48 It is impossible for sb. to do sth. ... Would it be possible to have a drink of water? Barry Lyndon (1975) 
It’s possible to learn this all by yourself.
Is it possible to get it under control? L62
Is it possible to train my dog to open the gate?

a serve as b 
Are you still able to serve as a police officer? A Time to Kill (1996) L43
This van would serve as my home. 
Teenage Badass (2020)
When my father was young, he served as the company's doctor.
If you put forward a plan, proposal, or name, you suggest that it should be considered for a particular purpose or job.

put forward a plan ... 
Who put forward this plan?
You must put forward a proposal and then talk to us.
[Story]: A better plan was put forward. 
was/how/when/who…

solve the problem of a 
The moment you solve the problem of ventilation, I will tell everyone about it. L60
We won’t get it under control, unless we solve the problem of ventilation. L62
We need to solve the problem so that... L59

# 2-65 Grammar
## should & ought to
### should do & ought to do 肯定: 
Leo will be alone tonight.
▪ We should invite Leo to our party.
▪ We ought to invite Leo to our party.

Your salary is very low. 
▪ You should look for another job. 
▪ You ought to look for another job. 
    
### should & ought to 否定: +not
▪ You shouldn’t believe him.
▪ You ought not to get out of the house. 
▪ You oughtn’t to call him that. 
▪ You ought not to eat that. You'll get fat. Places in the Heart (1984)

### should & ought to ⼀般疑问句: 提句⾸ 
▪ Should I call her? 
▪ Ought we to go back, Captain? Man in the Wilderness (1971)

### should have done / ought to have done 本应该做某事，但却没有。
▪ You missed a great party last night.
You should have come. Why didn’t you?
▪ You ought to have come.
▪ We lost the game yesterday, but we should have won.
We were the better team.
▪ We ought to have won.

should (ought to) do
should (ought to) have done
▪ You look tired. You should (ought to) go to bed now.
▪ You went to bed very late last night. 
You should (ought to) have gone to bed earlier.

# 2-66 Grammar
**have something done...**
**get something done...**
**含义: cause something to be done by somebody else.**

▪ I must have my watch repaired.
▪ I must get my watch repaired.
▪ I’m going to have my hair cut this afternoon.
▪ I’m going to get my hair cut this afternoon.

## 时态:
▪ I had my hair cut yesterday.
▪ We are having the house painted this week.
▪ I’m going to have my hair cut this afternoon.
▪ He has his hair cut every month.

## did vs have something done 
▪ Lisa repaired the roof.
▪ Lisa had the roof repaired.
▪ I made those curtains.
▪ I had those curtains made.
    
## have something done 
意外，不幸的遭遇。翻译成被。
Gary had his nose broken in a fight. 
I had my bag stolen.
Have you ever had your bike stolen?

# 2-67 Grammar

manage v.
Cambridge Dictionary:
to succeed in doing or dealing with something, especially something difficult 

## managed to do sth. = was able to do sth. 过去成功事件 (具体)

### 肯定句
1. The fire spread quickly, but everybody managed to / was able to escape.
2. They didn’t want to come with us at first, but we managed to / were able to persuade them.
3. I ran my first marathon recently, and it was very hard, but I managed to finish.

### managed to do sth. = was able to do sth. 
过去成功事件(具体) 
⼀般疑问句: Did ... manage to ...
I managed to tell him about this.
1. Did you manage to tell him about this?
I managed to find the remote control.
2. Did you manage to find the remote control?

# 2-69 Grammar
having been done...
完成被动!
▪After having been instructed to drive out of town, I began to acquire confidence.
▪After I had been instructed to drive out of town, I began to acquire confidence.

having been done...
▪He had been told many times.
▪He finally understood it.
▪After he had been told many times, he finally understood it.
▪Having been told many times, he finally understood it.

having been done...
▪They had been explained.
▪They agreed with me at last.
▪After they had been explained, they agreed with me at last.
▪Having been explained, they agreed with me at last.
      
having been done...
▪The fish had been caught by the fisherman.
▪The fish died very soon.
▪After the fish had been caught by the fisherman, the fish died very soon.
▪Having been caught by the fisherman, the fish died very soon.

# 2-73 Grammar
▪1. The climbers reached the top of 
the mountain. They spent the night 
there. (not only ... but ... as well) 
▪The climbers not only reached the 
top of the mountain, but (they) spent 
the night there as well. 
▪2. A fire broke out in a cinema. 
Several hundred people tried to leave 
the building. A number of them were 
injured. (When ... and) 
▪When a fire broke out in a cinema, 
several hundred people tried to leave 
the building and a number of them 
were injured. 
▪3. James Sullivan will give a lecture 
at the local library next week. His 
book on the Antarctic was published 
recently. (whose) 
▪James Sullivan, whose book on the 
Antarctic was published recently, will 
give a lecture at the local library next 
week. 
▪4. The police searched everywhere. 
The missing boy could not be found. 
His dog could not be found. (Although 
... neither ... nor) 
▪Although the police searched 
everywhere, neither the missing boy 
nor his dog could be found. 
▪5. Fares have increased. The railway 
company is still losing money. The 
employees have demanded higher 
wages. (In spite of the fact that ... 
because) 
▪In spite of the fact that fares have 
increased, the railway company is still 
losing money because the employees 
have demanded higher wages. 

▪6. He gave me a fright. I knocked the 
teapot over. (such ... that) 
▪He gave me such a fright that I 
knocked the teapot over. 
▪7. I made sure. The alarm clock 
worked. I set it. It would ring at six 
o’clock. (After making... that ... so 
that) 
▪After making sure that the alarm 
clock worked, I set it so that it would 
ring at six o'clock. 
▪8. I hid the Christmas presents under 
the desk quickly. My young daughter 
would not see them. She entered the 
room. (so that ... when) 
▪I hid the Christmas presents under 
the desk quickly so that my young 
daughter would not see them when 
she entered the room. 
▪9. I refused the offer. I explained. I 
had already been offered a job by 
another company. (Refusing ... that...) 
▪Refusing the offer, I explained that I 
had already been offered a job by 
another company. 
▪10. He fought the wolves off for three 
hours. Help arrived. (before) 
▪He fought the wolves off for three 
hours before help arrived.

# 2-77 Grammar
## ⾮限定性定语从句 
▪限定性定语从句 L28 
▪⾮限定性定语从句 L77: 解释，补充，说明。 
▪My mother, who is 50 this year, lives with me now.
▪Beijing, which is the capital of China, has developed into a big city. 
▪Among them will be Debbie’s mother, who swam the Channel herself… L36 
     
▪He has a daughter who works in a hospital.
▪He has a daughter, who works in a hospital. (=and she works in a hospital) 
## ⾮限定性定语从句 
▪as
1. 指代整个完整句⼦ 
2. 位置灵活: 前中后 (which只能放句尾) 
L14: As I soon learnt, he was English himself.
L74 As they soon learnt, disguises can sometimes be too perfect.

# 2-79 Grammar
## 部分倒装 
▪only 在句⾸，主谓(可能)颠倒。
▪Only on one occasion have I ever felt frightened.
▪Only then did I understand what she meant. 
▪Only after her death was I able to do that. 
       
Then, and only then, can the door be opened.
The Hobbit: An Unexpected Journey (2012)

"Only then will you understand what happened... and why."
A River Runs Through It (1992)

And only after a long time did I finally get my bearings. 
Another Woman (1988)

Only then will the world return to normal. 
Legend (1985)

Because only then, were we able to actually see the truth.      
Anna (2013)

## 部分倒装 
▪only 说明主语，主谓不倒装。
▪Only policemen can come in.
▪Only my mother really understands me.
